**6” chocolate chip cookie round** 

**Recipe Summary** 

**Yield:** 1 Batch Gross weight (total): 1.053 Kg Gross weight (per Batch): 1.053 Kg 

**Ingredient Quantities** 

**Ingredient Quantity** Butter, Room temp 120 g Large Eggs, Whole 1 each Large Eggs, Yolk 1 each Baking Vanilla 2 tsp Baking soda 1 tsp Salt 1 tsp Flour 300 g Chocolate Chips (large) 300 g Light brown sugar 200 g 

**Preparation Method** 

1\. Beat butter, and light brown sugar together on high until light and fluffy 2\. Add in eggs and vanilla until just combined 

3\. in separate mixing bowl, mix together baking soda, salt, and flour then add to wet mix 4\. Add in chocolate chips and mix 

5\. Place dough into respective molds and bake at proper temp for shape/size 

Weights & time: 

\<3- 300g @18min 

6” \-350g @18min 

8”- 700g @22min  
**Apple Chaider** 

**Recipe Summary** 

**Yield:** 1 batch Gross weight (total): 1 g Gross weight (per batch): 1 g 

*There are 2 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* 

**Ingredient Quantities** 

**Ingredient Quantity** Apple cider 2 qts Cinnamon sticks, full sticks 1 g Chai Tea (bagged) 5 each 

**Preparation Method** 

1\. pour cider into pot and add cinnamon sticks 

2\. Turn burner onto 8 and bring cider to a boil. Once boiling, turn to burner down to a 4 to create a simmer and cook till reduced by 1/3 

3.tirn off heat and add tea bags (balance tags on edge of pan), cover and steep for 5 minutes. make sure not to over steep, it will create bitter flavor 

4.while steeping, boil a plain pt of water and pour into a urn to preheat it. (use funnel to make easier) save water for later 

5\. pour chaider mix through strainer into a pitcher, pressing on the tea bags with a spatula to get all the liquid out. dispose of solids 

6\. pour out hot water from urn, pour in the chaider using funnel

**Banana Cake** 

**Recipe Summary** 

**Yield:** 2300 g Gross weight (total): 2.364 Kg Gross weight (per g): 1.028 g 

*There are 1 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* 

**Ingredient Quantities** 

**Ingredient Quantity** Butter, Room temp 160 g 

White Sugar 440 g 

Bananas, Ripe 500 g 

Sourcream 450 g 

Vanilla bean paste 2 tsp 

Large Eggs, Whole 4 each Baking soda 2 tsp 

Baking powder 2 tsp 

Salt 1.5 tsp Flour 560 g 

**Preparation Method** 

1\. In mixer cream together butter and sugar until light and fluffy 

2\. In separate bowl combine bananas and sour cream 

3\. Add two bowls together and mix 

4\. Add eggs in one at a time until combined fully 

5\. In separate bowl mix together flour, baking soda, baking powder and salt 

6\. Add dry ingredients to wet ingredients 

7\. Add batter (1150g per pan) to 9x13 pans that have been buttered and lined with parchment 8\. Bake and cool 

9\. After cooling crumble into small pieces and store in Cambro in cooler

| Butter | 70 g |
| Light brown sugar | 35 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Preheat oven to 325

2.Heat butter in small pan over medium temp until fully melted, continue cooking until butter becomes fragrant and golden (carefully not to burn)

4\. Return nuts to sheet trays to bake off for about 8 minutes 

5\. Remove from oven and continue to let dry and cool completely 

### **Brownie Ice-cream Sammie**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 2 × 9x13 pans |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 1.943 Kg |
| Gross weight (per 9x13 pans): | 971.488 g |
| *There are 1 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* |  |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Butter, Melted | 345 g |
| White Sugar | 450 g |
| Light brown sugar | 330 g |
| Large Eggs, Whole | 6 each |
| Canola oil | 3 tbsp |
| Baking Vanilla | 2 tbsp |
| Flour | 285 g |
| Cocoa Powder | 150 g |
| Baking soda | 1.5 tsp |
| Salt | 1.5 tsp |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. In pot melt butter down, 

2\. In standing mixer add in white sugar and brown sugar, add melted butter and whip on medium until light and fluffy 

3\. Add in eggs one at a time until fully incorporated, mix super well

4\. Mix in oil and vanilla

5\. In separate bowl combine flour, cocoa powder, baking soda, and salt 

6\. In intervals, add flour to wet mixture until all ingredients are not longer clumpy

7\. Evenly separate mixture into 2 9x13 pans (approx 825g per pan) , spread out evenly with tilted handle spatula 

8\. Bake for 15 min @325, 40% wind speed 

9\. Cool and store

### **Buckeyes**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 g |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 512.424 g |
| Gross weight (per g): | 512.424 g |
| *There are 1 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* |  |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Chocolate Chips (large) | 270 g |
| Coconut Oil | 1.5 tbsp |
| Peanut butter | 200 g |
| Powdered Sugar | 40 g |
| Sea Salt | 1/2 tsp |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Melt down chocolate chips and coconut oil in a double boiler 

2\. Spread evenly onto sheet pans and allow to freeze (10ish min)

3\. Mix together peanut butter and powdered sugar (sifted into pb) until no clumps 

4\. Spread pb mix on top of trays of chocolate prepped earlier

5\. Freeze again (10ish min)

6\. While waiting for pb mix to harden a bit, repeat step one and melt down more chocolate 

7\. Once chocolate is ready pour on top of pb layer and spread evenly 

8\. Freeze again until all layers are fully hardened 

9\. Chop into bite sized pieces and store in freezer

### **Caramelized White Chocolate**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 Serving |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 950 g |
| Gross weight (per Serving): | 950 g |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| White chocolate | 950 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

Roasting temp- 250\*F, 50% wind, 10min (to start)

1\. Add white chocolate evenly into deep trays 

2\.  Roast for 10 min

3\. Pull out and scrape the melted chocolate off sides and mix around

4\. Put back in and roast for 10 more minutes

5\. Repeat step 3, roast for 5 min 

6\. Repeat steps above until chocolate reaches peanut butter consistency and color 

7\. Add Caramelized white chocolate into cambro and allow to cool (uncovered) at room temp

8\. Once cooled, cover and store at room temp

2\. Melt butter and then toss in carrots

3\. Add in brown sugar and cinnamon 

4\. Once fully coated, place on gray and bake

5\. Let cool, then store in cambro in cooler

1.combine butter, white sugar, and dark brown sugar in mixing bowl and beat on high until light in color and fluffy

### **Chocolate cake (Scaled x 2100\)**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 2100 g |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 5.784 T |
| Gross weight (per g): | 2.754 Kg |
| *There are 1 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* |  |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| White Sugar | 1680000 g |
| Flour | 1050000 g |
| Cocoa Powder | 378000 g |
| Baking powder | 35700 g |
| Baking soda | 35700 g |
| Espresso Powder | 4200 tsp |
| Buttermilk | 4200 cups |
| Large Eggs | 8400 each |
| Canola oil | 2100 cups |
| Water, Boiling | 4200 cups |
| Baking Vanilla | 48300 g |
| Salt | 35700 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Combine flour,sugar,cocoa powder, baking powder, baking soda, salt, and espresso powder into mixer and beat on low until just barely combined

2\. Stream in buttermilk, vegetable oil, vanilla and eggs until combined fully 

3\. Slowly stream in boiling water and mix until, everything is combined 

4\. There will be lumps on the bottom that haven’t been incorporated so make sure you scrape down the sides and bottom of mixing bowl and beat again so everything is fully mixed together 

Weights:

\<3- 400g

6”-500g

8”- 900g

9x13- 1350g

### **GF Lemon Crinkle Cookies**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 batch |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 726.564 g |
| Gross weight (per batch): | 726.564 g |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| White Sugar | 150 g |
| Lemon, zested | 2 tbsp |
| Butter | 85 g |
| Baking Vanilla | 1/2 tsp |
| Large Eggs, whole | 2 each |
| Gluten Free Flour (bobs red mill) | 250 g |
| Baking powder | 1 tsp |
| Salt | 1/4 tsp |
| Powdered Sugar | 90 g |
| Lemon, juiced | 30 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Melt butter and allow to cool

2\. Massage together sugar and lemon zest to release the oils

3\. Combine Sugar, lemon zest with butter, vanilla, and lemon juice into standing mixer then cream together until light and fluffy

4\. Add eggs to wet mixture one at a time until everything is fully combined

5\. Add Gf flour, baking powder and salt in mixing bowl then slowly add into wet mixture while mixing

6\. Put dough into bowl and cover with plastic wrap, let sit in cooler for about a hour

7\. Scoop dough and roll in powder sugar 

8\. Place onto trays and store in freezer

5\. Spread onto trays in thick layer and cut into cubes once frozen

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Warm empty pot on medium/high heat, add in sugar a little bit at a time allowing it to melt before adding more

3\. Add in hazelnuts and salt, mix until fully combined

4\. Spread mix onto trays and allow to cool completely 

5\. Break apart once cooled and store at room temp in cambro

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Peaches | 1040000 g |
| Light brown sugar | 440000 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

Pealing peaches:

1\. Put pot of water on to boil, get a bowl of ice water ready

2\. Wash peaches and then dip into bowling water for 45sec/1 min depending on size of peach

3\. Immediately after bowling place into ice water bath to stop cooking

4\. Peel off skin and chop into pieces

Roasting: 

Bake at 325, 20% AS for 30min

### **Shortbread (Scaled x 2.763)**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1000 g |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 1.091 Kg |
| Gross weight (per g): | 1.091 g |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Butter, Room temp | 414.513 g |
| Powdered Sugar | 165.805 g |
| Salt | 1.382 tsp |
| Baking Vanilla | 1.382 tsp |
| Flour | 497.416 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1.beat butter on high until light and fluffy

2\. Add in powder sugar and salt cafefully and mix slowly

3\. Add in vanilla and beat on high again 

4\. Add in flour and and mix again

5\. Take 1500 grams in dough and form into long rectangle log about 12 in long and 3 in wide

6\. Place in cooler overnight

7\. Next day cut into even sized/thickness cookies and bake 

8\. Allow to cool for about 30 min then chop into small cubes and freeze

### **Lemon Curd**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 serving |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 343.328 g |
| Gross weight (per serving): | 343.328 g |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Large Eggs, Yolks | 72 g |
| Cornstarch | 1 tbsp |
| Lemon, juiced | 6.08 oz |
| Lemon, zested | 1 tbsp |
| White Sugar | 150 g |
| Butter | 113 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Separate egg yolks from whites (store whites for future use), add in cornstarch and  whisk vigorously until no clumps

2\. Combine lemon juice and zest with sugar and add to egg mixture

3\. Add to pot and cook on medium heat, stir frequently until very thick texture 

4\. Take off heat and add butter, mix until fully melted and combined 

5\. Put into cooling pan and cover the top of the curd to keep film from forming 

6\. Store in cooler

### **Meringue**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 qt |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 225.566 g |
| Gross weight (per qt): | 225.566 g |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Large Eggs, Whites only, room temp | 2 each |
| Cream of Tartar | 1/4 tsp |
| Sea Salt | 1/8 tsp |
| White Sugar | 110 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Separate egg whites from yolks, add whites to standing mixer and allow to come to room temp

2\. Whisk egg whites until frothy, then slowly add in cream or tartar and salt 

3\. Slowly add in sugar 

4\. Beat on high until shiny and fluffy

5\. Distribute between trays using piping bag to pipe small dollops onto parchment paper 

6\. Bake for 1 hour and let cool for 2 hours

7\. Remove from trays and store in freezer

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Melt dark chocolate with oils and spread onto trays and freeze

2\. Melt down white chocolate with oils, spread evenly on top of dark chocolate

3\. Sprinkle crushed peppermint candies over top of chocolate and freeze

4\. Once frozen fully, chop into pieces and store in cambro in freezer

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| White Sugar | 150 g |
| Large Eggs, Whole | 2 each |
| Molasses | 250 g |
| Wildflower Honey | 90 g |
| Orange Oil | 1/8 tsp |
| Flour | 285 g |
| Ginger | 1 tbsp |
| Cinnamon Flavor | 1.5 tsp |
| Baking soda | 2 tsp |
| Salt | 1 tsp |
| Water | 1/2 cups |
| Canola oil | 180 tsp |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Mix together veg oil, sugar, and eggs until thick and pale

2\. Stir in molasses, honey and orange oil

3\. Mix in flour, ginger, cinnamon, baking soda, and salt until just combined

4\. Add in water until fully incorporated 

5\. Bake and allow to cool

6\. Once cooled, crumble and store in cooler

### **Peanut brittle**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 620 g \[Grams (chopped)\] |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 763.693 g |
| Gross weight (per g \[Grams (chopped)\]): | 1.232 g |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| White Sugar | 200 g |
| Water | 60 g |
| Light corn syrup | 160 g |
| Butter | 115 g |
| Sea Salt | 1/4 tsp |
| Baking soda | 1/2 tsp |
| Honey Roasted Peanuts | 225 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Add sugar, water, corn syrup together into mixing bowl and combine, once combined add mix to pot and heat until it starts to boil 

2\. Add in butter and cook until it reaches dark amber color (290-300degrees)

3\. While base cooks prep the nuts by adding sea salt and baking soda to a bowl and mixing all together(set aside)

4\. When base is ready quickly add the nut mixture and transfer onto trays to smooth out

5\. Allow to come to room temp, chop into bite sized pieces and store in cambro at room temp

### **Pineapple Curd**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 serving |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 561.186 g |
| Gross weight (per serving): | 561.186 g |
| *There are 1 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* |  |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Large Eggs, Yolks only | 5 each |
| Cornstarch | 1.5 tbsp |
| Pineapple, juiced | 1 cup |
| Lemon, juiced | 0.25 oz |
| White Sugar | 150 g |
| Salt | 1/8 tsp |
| Butter | 113 g |
| Pineapple Gmf | 1/8 tsp |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Separate egg yolks from whites (store whites for future use), add in cornstarch and  whisk vigorously until no clumps

2\. Combine lemon juice and zest with sugar and add to egg mixture

3\. Add to pot and cook on medium heat, stir frequently until very thick texture 

4\. Take off heat and add butter, mix until fully melted and combined 

5\. Put into cooling pan and cover the top of the curd to keep film from forming 

6\. Store in cooler 1\. Separate egg yolks from whites (store whites for future use), add in cornstarch and  whisk vigorously until no clumps

2\. Combine pineapple juice and lemon juice with sugar and salt to egg mixture

3\. Add to pot and cook on medium heat, stir frequently until very thick texture 

4\. Take off heat and add butter, mix until fully melted and combined, add in pineapple extract

5\. Put into cooling pan and cover the top of the curd to keep film from forming 

6\. Store in cooler

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Butter, Room temp | 150 g |
| White Sugar | 265 g |
| Light brown sugar | 100 g |
| Canola oil | 1.5 cups |
| Pumpkin puree, Grams not tsp | 600 tsp |
| Vanilla bean paste | 2 tsp |
| Large Eggs, Whole egg | 3 each |
| Flour | 365 g |
| Baking soda | 1 tsp |
| Baking powder | 2 tsp |
| Salt | 1/2 tsp |
| Pumpkin pie spice mix | 22 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Combine butter and both sugars until light and fluffy 

2\. In separate bowl add oil, pumpkin purée, vanilla bean paste and eggs 

3\. Add wet ingredients to butter/sugar mix

4\. In separate bowl combine flour, baking soda, baking powder, salt, and pumpkin spice

5\. Add dry ingredients to wet ingredients and mix until just combined

Notes:

6”: 500g of batter @325, 40% for 20 min

8”: 900g of batter @325, 40% for 30 min

### **Pupsicles**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 serving |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 1.399 Kg |
| Gross weight (per serving): | 1.399 Kg |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Pumpkin puree | 620 g |
| Greek Yogurt | 620 g |
| Maple Syrup | 2 tbsp |
| Peanut butter | 120 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. In standing mixture beat the peanut butter on night until light and fluffy

2\. Add in maple syrup

3\. In small increments/inter changing, add pumpkin purée and yogurt until fully mixed

4\. Scrap down sides and mix again

5\. Using green handle scoop put mixture into each mold

6\. Once filled with mixture add popsicle stick into mold far into paw to line up with stick holder part on mold

7\. Tap tray onto table lightly to get rid of any air bubbles, top off each mold ass needed

8\. Scrape off top of each mold using offset spatula so they are flat on the backs, cover with parchment and freeze on trays in walk in freezer

9\. Remove from molds, add to bags with label and put out front for sale

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Beat together cream cheese and butter until fluffy

2\. Add flour and salt to bowl and mix until soft dough forms 

3\. Knead dough into a ball and wrap with plastic and form into rectangle 

4\. Chill dough for 8-24 hours 

5\. Cut dough into 4 pieces and roll into thin rectangles

6\. Cut those rectangles into triangles 

7\. Starting at the long end, roll away from you 

8\. At end of roll place a bit of milk to seal together

7\. Refrigerate for 2 hours before freezing pucks

#### **Preparation Method**

3\. Mix in flour,salt, baking soda and small portion of sprinkles until fully combined

4\. Scoop and roll in additional sprinkles, forming small round pucks into trays and freeze

5\. Once frozen place into ziploc bag and label properly

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| White Sugar | 100 g |
| Dark brown sugar | 100 g |
| Canola oil | 5 tbsp |

| Water | 1/2 cups |
| Gluten Free Flour (bobs red mill) | 250 g |
| Sea Salt | 1/2 tsp |
| Baking powder | 1 tsp |
| Baking soda | 1 tsp |
| Vegan Chocolate Chips | 175 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Add both sugars to a mixing bowl, while mixing slowly add in Canola oil and allow to mix until sugar is emulsified 

2\. Slowly add in water and vanilla until everything is well combined

3\. In mixing bowl combine all dry ingredients and mix well, slowly add them into the wet mixture until just combined

4\. Add in chocolate chips and mix again for just a moment

Now there are two ways we use these cookies:

5\. Scoop mix onto trays (green handle scoop) and form into hockey puck shape OR scoop into balls and cook off for vegan Cookie Monster 

6\. Either Store in freezer until fully frozen and then bag and label for foh cookies OR chop for vegan Cookie Monster

1\. Melt together vegan butter, corn syrup and maple syrup then allow to cool

2\. Separately, mix together corn flake crumbs, sugar and cinnamon

| Sea Salt | 1/4 tsp |
| Gluten Free Heat Treated Flour | 550 g |
| Enjoy Life- Vegan mini chocolate chips | 800 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Melt down butter and allow to cool slightly

2\. Add butter and sugar into standing mixer and cream together until light and fluffy

3\. Add vanilla and water into bowl slowly until combined fully

4\. Slowly add in sea salt and heat treated flour and mix until just combined 

5\. Toss in mini chocolate chips and mix until throughly distributed in batter

6\. Spread onto trays evenly about 1/4 inch thick 

7\. Freeze and then chop into bite sized pieces

8\. Store in freezer

5\. Add dry mix to wet mix, once fully mixed together scoop into balls 

6\. Lightly coat each cookie with sugar, shape into pucks and store in freezer

10\.  Store in freezer 

### **V/Gf Brownies**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 g |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 9.563 Kg |
| Gross weight (per g): | 9.563 Kg |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Vegan Butter, Melted | 226 g |
| White Sugar | 600 g |
| Baking Vanilla | 34 g |
| Egg Replacer | 40 g |
| Water | 3/4 cups |
| Gluten Free Flour (bobs red mill) | 240 g |
| Cocoa Powder | 130 g |
| Salt | 1/2 tsp |
| Vegan Chocolate Chips | 200 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. In standing mixer beat together butter, vanilla and sugar until light and fluffy 

2\. Separately mix together water and egg replacer until it thickens, add to butter mixture

3\. In another bowl, add together Gf flour, cocoa powder and salt, slowly add dry to wet mixture until just combined 

4\. Add in chocolate chips and mix until distributed equally 

5\. Evenly distribute batter (1550g) into 9x13 pans lined with butter and parchment 

6\. Allow to cool, trim off edges, cut into bite sized pieces 

7\. Store in freezer

### **V/GF Vanilla Cake**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 serving |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 1.652 Kg *(1.675 Kg before ingredient waste)* |
| Gross weight (per serving): | 1.652 Kg *(1.675 Kg before ingredient waste)* |
| *There are 1 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* |  |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| coconut milk | 560 ml |
| White Vinegar | 2.5 tsp |
| Canola oil | 3/4 cups |
| chickpeas (aquafaba), strain chickpeas and get the liquid out only | 2 tbsp |
| Baking Vanilla | 1 tbsp |
| Gluten Free Flour (bobs red mill) | 570 g |
| White Sugar | 380 g |
| Baking powder | 38 g |
| Salt | 1 tsp |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. combine coconut milk and vinegar in pitcher and set aside to curdle

2\.  in standing mixing bowl add oil, aquafaba, and vanilla and start on low

3\. while mixing add in mixture from step 1

4\. in separate mixing bowl sift in flour and sugar, then add in salt and baking powder

5\. add dry mix slowly to wet mix as it spins on low speed until just combined

6\. bake, allow to cool, cut tops off, store in freezer

### **Vegan Sun Butter Date Bark**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 g |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 972.424 g |
| Gross weight (per g): | 972.424 g |
| *There are 1 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* |  |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Dates, Pitted | 570 g |
| Sun butter | 150 g |
| Vegan Chocolate Chips | 250 g |
| Canola oil | 1 tbsp |
| Sea Salt | 1/2 tsp |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Pit dates

2\. Once dates are pitted, spread them out onto parchment paper and sand which them in with another piece of parchment paper, use rolling pin to flatten them out into thin sheets

3\. Transfer onto sheet trays, spread nut butter onto dates evenly using a spoon 

4\. Put in hardening cabinet to flash freeze

5\. Melt down chocolate with oil in double boiler 

6\. Once fully melted, remove the sheets from freezer and pour chocolate mixture on top of sunbutter layer 

7\. Sprinkle light layer of sea salt evenly across the top of chocolate and freeze

8\. Once fully frozen chop into bite sized pieces 

9\. Store in freezer

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 3 Waffles |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 2.736 Kg |
| Gross weight (per Waffle): | 912.109 g |
| *There are 1 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* |  |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| coconut milk | 1.5 cups |
| Apple Cider Vinegar | 2 tsp |
| Baking Vanilla | 1/2 tsp |
| Flour | 280 g |
| Baking powder | 1 tbsp |
| Vegan Butter, Melted | 57 g |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Combine coconut milk, vanilla and vinegar, set aside and allow to curdle

2\. Sift together flour, baking powder and salt, set aside 

3\. Add brown sugar and butter to coconut milk mixture 

4\. Fold in dry mixture until just combined 

5\. Preheat waffle maker (350\*) and grease lightly with vegan butter 

6\. Add 2 green scoops worth of batter and allow to cook for 2 min 30 sec

7\. Open waffle maker and remove waffle

8\. Freeze 

(Reheat: 200\* 30% wind speed, 8ish min)

### **Wet Cinnamon Walnuts**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 Serving |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 948.662 g |
| Gross weight (per Serving): | 948.662 g |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| White Sugar | 200 g |
| Light corn syrup | 300 g |
| Water | 1 cup |
| Walnuts | 200 g |
| Baking Vanilla | 1.5 tsp |
| Cinnamon (powdered) | 1 tsp |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. In a sauce pan combine sugar, water, and corn syrup on medium heat. Stir continuously till sugar is dissolved and the corn syrup is clear

2\. Cook and stir till mixture starts to boil, let boil for about 4 minutes without stirring

3\. Add in walnuts and vanilla to the pan, cook and stir till mixture starts to boil, stir consistency will boiling for 4 minutes, remove from heat and cool 

4\. Store wet walnuts at room temp

### **Zucchini Paste**

#### **Recipe Summary**

| Yield: | 1 serving |
| ----- | :---- |
| Gross weight (total): | 37.535 g |
| Gross weight (per serving): | 37.535 g |
| *There are 3 food ingredients that are not included in the weight calculations as they have no weight-based measurement converter.* |  |

#### **Ingredient Quantities**

| Ingredient | Quantity |
| ----- | ----- |
| Zucchini, grated | 450 g |
| Butter | 35 g |
| Salt | 1/4 tsp |
| Cinnamon (powdered) | 1/4 tsp |
| Cloves | 1/4 tsp |
| Nutmeg | 1/4 tsp |
| Pepper | 1/4 tsp |
| Coriander | 1/4 tsp |

#### **Preparation Method**

1\. Melt butter in a pot

2\. Add in zucchini and salt to butter, reduce slightly

3\. Add in spice mixture and cook down until loose paste forms and it slightly browns
